## 0. Mathematical verdict

**Not solved.**

The fixed goal is to prove `lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ=1`, uniformly over all sufficiently large horizons and every predetermined nonnegative word, or rigorously disprove it by a full-class asymptotic separation. Here `W_n(h)=sup 2(f(x_n)-f_star)/(L||x_0-x_star||^2)` over every finite dimension, convex L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct start; `W=2R`. The exact benchmark is `U_n^ZJ=1/B_n`, with all integer splits in the supplied Zhang--Jiang recurrence. Neither the target nor its quantifiers change.

Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` closes the exact benchmark comparison and conditional separation threshold; `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` closes finite-word projection-simplex transfer and the actual H3 law; `b98bb807d514e80d` closes the single-cut portfolio at its stated scope. None proves the target. A candidate upper architecture is a retained PD join whose full-class history input remains unproved. Its quantitative scalar margin is derived, but does not establish that input.

The latest draft supplies an actual method failure: a weak-history composition retaining at least the whole weak left residual is impossible. It does not supply proposition failure: on that same trajectory the requested parent history remains strictly positive. A separate fixed-depth coercivity proof restricts a possible violation to internal depths growing with the enclosing depth. This is not a uniform all-depth result. PARTIAL and SUBSTANTIAL do not mean almost solved; the unmatched interface is still a theorem about the full original class.

| Item | Mathematical state |
|---|---|
| Fixed goal | UNCHANGED: exact limit one or conclusive full-class asymptotic disproof |
| Verified complete proof | NO |
| Verified counterexample to the original claim | NO |
| Signed-closed supporting components | Three scoped components: benchmark; projection transfer/H3; single-cut portfolio |
| Main missing statement | A uniform original-radius lower witness, or an all-depth full-class upper certificate |
| False or obsolete methods | YES: unit weak-left retention, named positive interval cones, A/B source profiles, specified inverse greedy and monotone-face policies |
| Highest-priority unresolved task | P0: close the weak history input using a genuine whole-tree or cross-subtree certificate |

| Sub-task | Status | Closed facts | Conditional or draft material | Main missing interface |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Exact benchmark and normalization | CLOSED | `931b9653ce0f7201` | No new premise for its own statement | None within its accepted scope |
| Finite-word projection transfer | CLOSED | `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` | Higher U laws are not implied | Uniform higher-depth inequality |
| Low-mass all-word lower region | CLOSED | `b98bb807d514e80d` | Huber bound `1/(1+2 sum h)` | Complementary high-mass words |
| High-mass exact-profile lower bound | PARTIAL | No closing fact | Inverse and coupled-face mechanisms with scoped obstructions | One legal original-radius all-word construction |
| Separating upper bound | SUBSTANTIAL | Benchmark implication only | Conditional PD join and genuine leaf inputs | Weak history at every inherited depth |

Current best proof skeleton:

1. Keep `C_0=B_0=1`, `C_n=max Omega(C_k,C_l)` and `B_n=max G(C_k,B_l)` over ALL k+l=n-1, with `Omega=(x+y+sqrt(x^2+6xy+y^2))/2` and `G=2x+y/2+sqrt(y^2+8xy)/2`.
2. The supplied Zhang--Jiang schedule gives `W_n^*<=1/B_n`.
3. Accepted `b98bb807d514e80d` provides the matching lower bound whenever `sum h<=(B_n-1)/2`. The other words remain uncovered.
4. Independently, keep the literal perturbed hierarchy defined below, with no child parameter reset.
5. Prove its displayed weak history input for all required inherited children; this is the central missing lemma.
6. The retained PD identity and its actual leaf then produce a full-class dominance certificate of total `1+(32/17)S_m`.
7. This yields `W(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m]<=17/[16(1+2S_m)]` at every original selected radius.
8. Accepted `931b9653ce0f7201` then gives a strict `799/800` subsequence comparison and resolves the original question negatively. Steps 5--7 are not established.

Central missing lemma: for every sufficiently large integer m, set `epsilon=1/(4m)`, `beta=1-epsilon`, `c=(1+sqrt(2))(1+epsilon)`, `a=beta/c`, `H_0=()`, `H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j)`, `S_j=sum H_j`, `C_j=1+S_j`. For EVERY 1<=j<m and every original convex 1-smooth actual H_j trajectory, prove

    E_j = 2 sum h_i(f_i-f_end) - sum h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||^2
          - [(7/10) C_j^2-C_j] ||g_end||^2 >= 0.

All children inherit the same enclosing m. The conditional joining calculation, fixed-depth strict positivity and Huber calibrations do not prove this assertion. The alternative affirmative route still needs a uniform high-mass lower witness at the exact B_n profile.

## 1. Closed components and obsolete routes

Signed-closed components:

- Exact all-integer benchmark comparison and conditional full-class separation threshold — `931b9653ce0f7201`.
- Fixed-word projection-simplex supremum transfer and the actual same-parameter H3 U law — `1e0bf3f41457d8e4`.
- Exact single-cut Huber portfolio and its precise insufficiency — `b98bb807d514e80d`.

Every new result in the following table is a manual draft, not a fact-graph acceptance. Full statements and derivations are in R13_FINAL_HANDOFF_20261009.md and the two complete source proofs it identifies.

|---|---|---|
| Unit or larger weak-left retention plus nonnegative residuals/rows/PSD | FALSE AS STATED | Actual asymmetric Huber gives E_P<E_L at every internal j>=3; even an unrestricted PSD remainder cannot repair that inequality |
| Positive interval-mode cone for fixed increasing physical weights | FALSE AS STATED | Actual column and full diagonal conditions force `1+r^3<=1+r/6`, but `6r^2>1`, r=sqrt(2)-1 |
| Fixed-depth negative history followed by absorption as m tends to infinity | FALSE AS STATED | Strict fixed-depth Silver coercivity transfers to all sufficiently large enclosing m |
| Farther-original-minimizer absorption for cone projections | FALSE AS STATED | Every physical prefix remains within its original distance to every original polar minimizer |
| Critical Huber saturated outer lift with arbitrary permitted inner phase at floor(m/2) | OBSOLETE | Its prescribed increasing-profile residual has a uniform positive order-S_m^2 margin |
| Fixed decreasing profiles A and B | FALSE AS STATED | Prior actual complete odd-depth Huber runs have negative D_u for every admissible certificate matrix |
| Specified inverse variance greedy and nested-face reserve strategies | FALSE AS STATED | Prior full trajectory costs or exact nested-face ceilings fail the benchmark |

General nonseparable matrices, growing-depth nonhomogeneous trajectories, and the original proposition are not excluded by these statements.

## 2. Interface contract table

### Weak history to the original separating upper bound

**Input required.** The exact central missing lemma for every inherited child, together with the genuine full-class leaf dominance input. **Output claimed.** Fixed nonnegative dominance weights of total `1+(32/17)S_m`, hence the original-radius upper bound and separation. **Available facts.** `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies only the final benchmark implication. **Missing verification on the actual model.** The all-depth history inequality itself; after it is proved, a finished-object audit of the entire implication. **Failure mode if ignored.** Primitive positivity at perturbed higher levels would be assumed rather than proved. **Status.** SUBSTANTIAL.

### Exact Silver coercivity to perturbed growing depth

**Input required.** At epsilon zero the leaf and original-row join imply standard history positivity; equality with zero terminal gradient forces all gradients zero. **Output claimed.** For each FIXED j, some eta_j>0 and M_j<infinity give `E_j>=(eta_j/2) sum ||g_i||^2` for every m>=M_j and every finite-dimensional actual history. **Available facts.** No fact graph entry certifies this new draft. **Missing verification on the actual model.** Quantitative constants controlling j growing with m, or another uniform argument. **Failure mode if ignored.** Exchanging `for each j, eventually m` with `eventually m, for all j<m` falsely closes the goal. **Status.** PARTIAL.

### A non-unit weak-history join to all-depth preservation

**Input required.** Actual interpolation rows connecting original left and right queries, and full child inequalities with exactly their warranted coefficients. **Output claimed.** A preservation rule allowing a weak-left coefficient below one while restoring the prescribed physical function-value coefficients and proving the complete remainder nonnegative. **Available facts.** None supplies this construction; R13's actual obstruction only excludes coefficient at least one. **Missing verification on the actual model.** Actual coefficients, every row sign, the full gradient-space remainder, the leaf and a uniform induction. **Failure mode if ignored.** A missing function-value coefficient or a signed temporal-moment term can invalidate the claimed identity. **Status.** UNKNOWN.

### Full increasing-profile matrix to a dominance certificate

**Input required.** S=S_m, T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, `u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i` before the terminal query, `u_n=1+(32/17)S-sum u_i`; an actual zero-row-sum C with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and columns b_i=u_i, b_n=-sum_(i<n)u_i. With Bmat the actual trajectory matrix and L the graph Laplacian of weights `u_j(u_i-h_i)`, require FULL PSD of `R=L+diag(u_i(1-u_i)+b_i-L_ii)-(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T)`. **Output claimed.** Nonnegative original dominance residual and the same separating upper bound. **Available facts.** `931b9653ce0f7201` only after the upper premise. **Missing verification on the actual model.** Actual C outside the excluded families and the full uniform PSD proof. **Failure mode if ignored.** A positive Laplacian is only one summand; the whole positive interval cone already fails the full diagonal. **Status.** PARTIAL.

### Legal high-mass witness to an affirmative exact-profile bound

**Input required.** One static admissible function and complete actual trajectory for every high-mass nonnegative word, including zero and arbitrarily large finite steps, at the original selected radius. **Output claimed.** `W_n(h)>=(1-o(1))/B_n` uniformly in h. **Available facts.** `b98bb807d514e80d` closes only the complementary low-mass region. **Missing verification on the actual model.** A globally coupled constant-preserving construction and an all-word selection rule. **Failure mode if ignored.** Changing a wall can change a previously recorded gradient; using another minimizer can silently replace the original radius. **Status.** PARTIAL.

## 3. Dangerous heuristic lines and strategies not to pursue

“The weak history holds at every fixed depth, so it holds at j=m.” **DANGEROUS.** The compactness constants depend on j without a bound. Growing depth is the missing content, not an endpoint convention.

“Retain the weak left residual in full and repair the bridge by more positive rows.” **FALSE AS STATED.** The actual Huber construction has E_P<E_L. This excludes even arbitrary positive original rows and a full PSD remainder under that retention coefficient.

“The fixed increasing weights have a positive Laplacian, so diagonal compensation can be tuned inside the interval cone.” **FALSE AS STATED.** The new obstruction uses the FULL diagonal after the Laplacian cancellation. General matrices remain outside its scope.

“A positive Huber phase test proves the profile.” **DANGEROUS.** The complete covered Huber family has a positive margin, but other starts, functions and dimensions are not included.

“A cone distance bound proves the weak history input.” **DANGEROUS.** The cone argument rules out a specific absorbed negative-history mechanism; it does not establish the terminal objective and gradient terms for arbitrary functions.

- Do not repeat the unit-left weak-history join with different bridge/right coefficients: the actual obstruction covers all such nonnegative representations.
- Do not retune a positive interval-mode combination for the fixed increasing profile: the entire named cone fails.
- Do not use a bounded internal-depth scan or continuity limit as a uniform induction.
- Do not call a free negative matrix direction an actual smooth-convex trajectory.
- Do not restart any child with new enclosing parameters or replace its original minimizer/radius.
- Do not promote the conditional PD identity, exact benchmark implication, or a scoped draft exclusion to the original answer.

## 4. Missing bridge lemmas

### Bridge 1 — close the weak history at growing inherited depth

**Target statement.** The exact central missing lemma in Section 0, for all sufficiently large m and every 1<=j<m. **Prerequisites.** Original nonnegative interpolation rows, true child coefficients and same enclosing parameters. **Existing facts to use.** `931b9653ce0f7201` supplies the consequence only; no accepted fact supplies the new input. **Missing checks.** A non-unit left-retention construction with actual cross-subtree rows, or a quantitative whole-tree bound on the retained signed defects, with explicit constants sufficient for the three-tenths terminal loss. **Closure criterion.** A complete dimension-uniform original-space inequality covering all required j, followed by the retained PD induction. This is the primary upper-route bridge; it is independent of the affirmative lower construction.

### Bridge 2 — instantiate a full global dominance certificate

**Target statement.** For all sufficiently large m, construct the exact increasing-profile C and prove the complete R from Section 2 PSD, or supply another fully specified original-radius dominance certificate of the same sufficient total. **Prerequisites.** The actual physical trajectory matrix, all column/row constraints and nonnegative interpolation coefficients. **Existing facts to use.** `931b9653ce0f7201` for the final comparison. **Missing checks.** Genuine coefficients outside the excluded positive interval and single-destination families; full positivity rather than separate convenient signs. **Closure criterion.** The full original-class upper bound, not its matrix reformulation. This is an independent sufficient upper route.

### Bridge 3 — attain the exact lower profile on every high-mass word

**Target statement.** For every eta>0, all sufficiently large n and every nonnegative word with `sum h>(B_n-1)/2`, construct an original admissible instance with payoff at least `(1-eta)/B_n`, with threshold independent of h. **Prerequisites.** One final fixed function, every physical query and the original radius; correlations must survive all face updates. **Existing facts to use.** `b98bb807d514e80d` for the complementary words and `1252b8823bb5a290` for full interpolation scope. **Missing checks.** A globally coupled witness cost and a constant-preserving uniform selection rule. **Closure criterion.** Every quantifier of the original lower bound follows. This is independent of the two upper routes and remains theorem-strength.

# A prospective quadratic-occupation common column

UNVERIFIED MAIN ANALYTICAL LEAD. No mathematical program or formal call was used. This document does not reassign a live worker or authorize another invocation. It is a possible next mechanism ONLY after the full global-cut report is read and the current invocation is actually terminal. Original minimax Goal OPEN.

Retain the full free-pi notation of the current original-array architecture: Ltri lower triangular, t=Ltri e, h=H_m, n=2^m-1, l=n-1, s=product_(i<n)(h_i-1)>0, q=17/(16D), pi=sqrt(q), E=pi+s, F=pi-s, eta=F/E. Put b=1+a, g=1+beta, and define the unit-curvature PREFIX column

 w_i=product_(j<i)(1-h_j), so Ltri w=e and w_n=-s.

The signed occupation weights

 p_i=h_i*w_i/(1+s), i<n,

sum to1 by telescoping w_i-w_(i+1)=h_i*w_i. They are signed, not probabilities. Write

 Tquad=sum_(i<n)h_i*w_i^2,
 Mclock=sum_(i<n)t_i*h_i*w_i,
 p0=b/(1+s).

Define a zero-sum column with zero ORIGINAL source coordinate by

 u=p-p0*e0-(1-p0)*e1.

Then e^T u=0, u_0=0, and the two exact scalar products should be rederived before use:

 w^T u=(Tquad-b^2)/(1+s)+a,
 t^T u=Mclock/(1+s)-(1+b)+b^2/(1+s).

Let r=l-1, the actual penultimate query. Its clock gap to the last query is t_l-t_r=g. Set

 uhat=u-[(t^T u)/g]*(e_l-e_r).

This leaves e^T uhat=0 and uhat_0=0, and makes t^T uhat=0 exactly. Since w_l=s/a and w_r=-s/(a*beta),

 w^T uhat=(Tquad-b^2)/(1+s)+a-[s/(a*beta)]*(t^T u).

Thus imposing the clock condition at the two last original queries changes the quadratic-prefix product only by a factor containing the extremely small full product s. It does NOT introduce a physical step or change any gradient trajectory.

Starting from the old source-optimal column zsrc=(E/a)(b e0-e1), consider the ONE explicit new column

 gamma=q*(1+s)/(2*(q-s^2)),
 znew=zsrc+E*gamma*uhat.

The denominator is positive if s<pi. This candidate preserves EXACTLY

 e^T znew=E,
 t^T znew=-E/a,
 (znew)_0=E*b/a.

Consequently the original Xreq=D-pi^(-2)-eta*(t^T z)^2, source charge calibration, source capacity and source row-clock moment formulas should remain unchanged, provided the complete original polynomial and skew moments are recomputed with this znew. No finite-entry sign conclusion follows from those identities.

The reason for this precise gamma is the proposed global quadratic-prefix cost:

 Tw=(w^2)^T mu+2alpha^T(w^2)-4s*v^T w-4F*z^T w,
 mu=2pi Ltri^T v.

The before-terminal-pool part of (w^2)^T mu contains exactly2q*Tquad. The displayed gamma makes the Tquad contribution of -4F*znew^T w exactly-2q*Tquad, because F*E=q-s^2. This is an algebraic cancellation lead; all tail-pool corrections and all other terms still require a uniform exact bound. The sign of Tw has NOT been established in this advisory.

Remaining obligations before this can be a candidate certificate include:

# R10 parent note: actual inverse cost and future-preserving wall repair

2026-10-09. **UNVERIFIED manual research draft. Original target OPEN.** This is a concrete extension of the finite-law inverse construction, not an all-word minimax theorem. No mathematical program, solver or formal submission was used. It was derived independently by the parent while two bounded R10 native calls were running. Their original allocations are not increased by this note.

## 1. One fixed finite body with variable lower walls

On a finite space with positive weights p_i, let E be the Euclidean space of mean-zero real variables, with inner product E[XY]. Fix a nonnegative variable a with mean a_bar>0 and define

    K_a={g in E: g_i>=-a_i for every i},
    F_a(X)=max_(g in K_a) E[Xg]-E[g^2]/2.

K_a is nonempty, compact and convex; it contains0. F_a is convex1-smooth, has gradient P_(K_a), and attains its minimum0 at the original selected point0. Positive walls give a unique minimum; uniqueness is not needed below. The initial squared radius relative to this selected minimum is E[X0^2], with no replacement by a later radius. A fixed weighted Euclidean isometry identifies this with an ordinary finite-dimensional instance.

The physical projection for a mean-zero X is

    g_i=max(X_i-t,-a_i),   E g=0,

where t>=0. Equivalently E(X-t+a)_+=a_bar. The actual update remains X'=X-hg. The wall labels a, all weights and the final body are fixed before the forward trajectory. A backward atom split duplicates the entire already-built future record, including its current wall label, before an explicitly justified modification.

## 2. Exact inverse formulas, including the wall cross moment

Take h=1+d>1, a mean-zero next law Y, and a real t satisfying

    Z=t+d*a-Y>=0.

Choose a right-branch probability omega_i in[0,1] with E[omega Z]=d*a_bar. The two inverse values are

    X_L=Y-h*a=t-a-Z,
    X_R=t-a+Z/d.

Their projection gradients are -a and -a+Z/d, respectively. Their weighted mean is0, as is their gradient mean, and both physical h updates give the same Y. Thus these are actual inverse points of one projection body. The mean-zero condition also forces t>=0.

Put J=E[omega Z^2], J_a=E[omega a Z], and M_a=E[a^2]. Direct expansion gives

    Var X=Var Y-2h E[aY]+h^2 M_a+2h*t*a_bar
                         -(2h/d)J_a+(d^-2-1)J,
    F_a(X)=t*a_bar+(d+1/2)M_a-E[aY]-(h/d)J_a+J/(2d^2),
    ||P_(K_a)X||^2=M_a-(2/d)J_a+J/d^2.

For the value formula, write X-g=t on the upper branch and X-g=t-Z on the lower branch. Since E g=0, F_a(X)=E[g^2]/2+E[(1-omega)aZ]. This proves the displayed formula without a missing normal contribution.

For nonnegative LOCAL cost coefficients alpha,beta (not the benchmark sequences), define

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)=Var X+2alpha F_a(X)+beta||P_(K_a)X||^2,
    T=h+alpha, C0=1+alpha+beta,
    K0=h^2+alpha(2d+1)+beta.

Then the actual full cost is

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)
     =Var Y-2T E[aY]+K0 M_a+2T*t*a_bar
       -[2(h(1+alpha)+beta)/d]J_a+(C0/d^2-1)J.       (1)

The wall cross moment is real data, not a free scalar reserve.

## 3. The same primitive inverse-cost identity survives

Assume C0>d^2. Define

    w=C0/d^2-1>0, gamma=d+2T/w,
    R=Y+gamma*a.

Choose the unique z with E(R-z)_+=gamma*a_bar. Since E R=gamma*a_bar, one has z>=0. Select all atoms with R>z, optionally a fraction of boundary atoms with R=z, and none with R<z. Let I denote this possibly split selected set, p=P(I)>0, a_I=E[a*1_I]. Set

    t=z+(d+gamma)(a_bar-a_I)/p.                    (2)

This t is nonnegative and gives E[1_I Z]=d*a_bar. It is an admissible inverse threshold if and only if the actual support guard

    t>=max_i(Y_i-d*a_i)                            (3)

holds. Unselected atoms already satisfy (3), since R_i<=z and a_i>=0. Selected atoms still need it. No physical child is rescaled in this construction.

Write U=(R-z)_+. The auxiliary SAME-body projection is

    q=P_(K_a)(Y/gamma)=U/gamma-a.

From w( gamma-d )=2T and direct substitution,

    2(h(1+alpha)+beta)/d=w(d+gamma),
    K0=w*gamma*d.

Therefore the last two terms of (1) combine as w E[1_I Z(t-R)]. Using E U=gamma*a_bar gives

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)
     =Var Y-2T E[aY]+K0 M_a+2T*z*a_bar
                        -w(d+gamma)E[aU]+w E[U^2].             (4)

Also, by expanding the actual q,

    U_gamma(Y):=2gamma F_a(Y/gamma)
       =2z*a_bar+E[U^2]/gamma-2E[aY]-gamma*M_a,
    ||q||^2=E[U^2]/gamma^2-2E[aU]/gamma+M_a.

Consequently (4) is exactly

    C_(alpha,beta)(X)=Var Y+T U_gamma(Y)+B_gamma||q||^2,
    B_gamma=w gamma^2-T gamma=gamma(wd+T)>0.         (5)

Thus the guarded identity from R9 is not limited to equal lower walls. In particular alpha=C_L-1, beta=C_L(C_L-1) and d(C_L+C_R+d)=C_L*C_R yield gamma=C_R and B_gamma=(C_L+d)(C_L+C_R+d), exactly as in the R9 parent note. This remains a conditional attained identity, not a closed composition theorem.

## 4. A genuine future-preserving repair of the support guard

Suppose an entire finite future physical trajectory has already been built with this body, starting at Y and including every future terminal evaluation. Suppose EACH selected atom i in I is unsaturated at every such query: its recorded gradient satisfies

    g_i^(k)=X_i^(k)-t_k

for every future query k. Equality at the clipping boundary is allowed; a strictly clipped lower branch is not.

Increase a_i, only on selected atoms, to any larger finite values. Each old unsaturated projection formula remains unchanged at every future query: max(X_i^(k)-t_k,-a_i) still equals the recorded g_i^(k). Other atoms and all means remain unchanged. Therefore every recorded t_k still solves the projection equation, all future actual gradients/iterates and objective values stay EXACTLY the same. The terminal objective is preserved, and0 remains a legal selected minimizer.

The auxiliary projection q in Section3 is also unchanged. On selected atoms U_i increases by gamma times the wall increase; both E U and gamma*a_bar increase equally. Thus z, selected massp and the set I can be kept fixed (a selected boundary atom may become strictly selected). Because a_bar-a_I is unchanged, (2) keeps the SAME t. On each increased atom Y_i-d*a_i decreases, while unselected atoms already satisfy (3). Choose

    new a_i>=max(old a_i,(Y_i-t)/d)

on the selected atoms. This makes the support guard hold everywhere. Formula (5) then constructs an actual predecessor, at the SAME cost on its right-hand side: VarY, q and U_gamma(Y) did not change.

All choices can be made backwards before the actual forward run. Atom duplication preserves its whole recorded future, and the change above is justified by every recorded query, not only its endpoint. The terminal function is one fixed K_a after all changes; there is no function change during execution.

This is a zero-additional-cost guard repair under an explicit persistent-unsaturation condition. It is NOT permission to increase the wall of an atom that was strictly saturated at any already-built future query. At such a query the old gradient generally changes. Nor does it prove that the selected upper-tail atoms at the next inverse step enjoy the condition.

The hypothesis can be weakened to just the selected atoms that actually require an increase: those with Y_i-d*a_i>t. All other selected walls can be left fixed. Such a violating atom necessarily has Y_i>0, since t>=0 and a_i>=0. This observation narrows the required persistent-unsaturation property; it does not prove it after a reflected inverse branch.

If an already constructed proof also relies on other auxiliary evaluations, their projection records must be included among the samples preserved by this operation, or their changed contributions must be paid separately. Preserving the physical future alone preserves its objective and chronology, but does not automatically preserve a previous auxiliary-potential bookkeeping argument.

## 5. The actual remaining composition requirement

An all-word or full-H_m construction must preserve enough persistent-unsaturated atoms to perform Section4 at every needed inverse join, while controlling the entire prefix cost and terminal scale. The current note does not prove that invariant, nor give an original variance bound at the benchmark scale. It does identify an actual operation that repairs the previous real guard while preserving all built future data; it is stronger than renaming that guard.

Any attempt using this operation must show, for every selected atom and every later physical query, why no strict lower saturation occurred. A high terminal value alone does not imply that history property. If the needed selected atom has already saturated, the free repair is unavailable and the cost of any alternative must be paid explicitly.

No original minimax conclusion, full-class upper bound, formal acceptance, or permission to renew old grants follows. The note is an UNVERIFIED lead for a fresh bounded direct construction, and all original target quantifiers remain unchanged.

# Unallocated R11 lead: a non-diagonal Brownian-bridge remainder for dominance

**UNVERIFIED manual algebra. No full certificate, model allocation, mathematical program or formal submission. Original target OPEN.** This note preserves a genuinely non-diagonal remainder and changes the function-value weights; the positive-diagonal interval dictionary excluded in R11_VARIABLE_INTERVAL_CERTIFICATE_LEAD_20261009.md does not automatically cover it. Its actual coefficient/positivity problem is UNSOLVED.

## 1. Exact original-radius dominance residual

Fix an arbitrary physical word h_0,...,h_(n-1)>=0 and its actual normalized smooth-convex trajectory. All sampled gradients include the actual terminalg_n. Use

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||²>=0,
    Q_star,i=2fstar-2f_i+2<g_i,x_i-xstar>-||g_i||²>=0.

Choose fixed nonnegative weightsu_0,...,u_n with totalB=sum u_i>0. This local B is a certificate weight, distinct from the original benchmark B_n. Define the doubled dominance residual

    D_u=||x0-xstar||²-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2B(f_n-fstar)-||x0-xstar-sum_i u_i g_i||².

It is exactly

    D_u=2sum_i u_i(f_i-f_n)
         +sum_i u_i(1-u_i)||g_i||²
         +2sum_(j<i)u_i(h_j-u_j)<g_i,g_j>.             (1)

Every j<i satisfiesj<n, so h_j in(1) is always an actual physical step. If D_u>=0 for ALL actual smooth-convex trajectories, then nonnegativity ofQ_star,i and of the final square gives the full-class bound W_n(h)<=1/B, at the original selected minimizer/radius. No such nonnegativity is assumed.

## 2. A covariance that represents the cross-gradient terms exactly

Choose parameters

    1>rho_0>rho_1>...>rho_n>0,
    u_i=rho_i h_i for i<n.

The strict ordering is convenient for a nonsingular covariance; weak ordering is possible with degeneracies treated explicitly. Chooseu_n>0 separately. Introduce scalar weightsell_i=h_i fori<n andell_n=u_n/rho_n. The LASTell_n is solely an algebraic weight, NOT a physical step or another update.

Define the matrix

    K_ij=ell_i ell_j [min(rho_i,rho_j)-rho_i rho_j].   (2)

It is positive semidefinite, since

    sum_(i,j)K_ij<g_i,g_j>
      =integral_0^1 ||sum_i ell_i g_i
                   (1_(t<=rho_i)-rho_i)||² dt >=0.   (3)

This is the covariance of a centered Bernoulli-threshold process, often called the Brownian-bridge covariance. Formula(3) proves the PSD statement directly; no external stochastic theorem is needed.

Fori>j, rho_i<rho_j implies

    K_ij=ell_i ell_j rho_i(1-rho_j)=u_i(h_j-u_j).

Its diagonal isK_ii=u_i(ell_i-u_i). Therefore the exact same functional(1) can be written

    D_u=2sum_(i<n)u_i(f_i-f_n)
        +sum_i u_i(1-ell_i)||g_i||²
        +sum_(i,j)K_ij<g_i,g_j>.                     (4)

This does not replace the physical gradients by random ones. The covariance is a deterministic positive matrix acting on the ORIGINAL queried gradient vectors.

## 3. Combine with original interpolation rows without forcing diagonal slack

Let Bmat be the actual trajectory matrix with diagonal1 and Bmat_ij=h_j forj<i. Suppose C is a zero-row-sum matrix with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and column sums

    b_i=u_i fori<n, b_n=-sum_(i<n)u_i=u_n-B.

Then, usinglambda_ij=C_ji fori!=j,

    sum_(i,j)C_ji Q_ij
       =2sum_i b_i f_i+g^T(H-diag(b))g,
    H=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T.

Consequently the EXACT remaining matrix is

    D_u=sum_(i,j)C_ji Q_ij+g^T R g,
    R=K+diag(u_i(1-ell_i)+b_i)-H.                    (5)

For a word satisfying the companion interval modes' h_i>=1 assumptions, those modes make H diagonal, soR=K+diag(mu), but the diagonalmu is NOT required to be nonnegative. The complete R must be proved PSD. This distinction matters: imposingmu>=0 throws away the non-diagonal mechanism and returns to a much smaller method.

Formula(3) gives an exact alternative route to that matrix proof: partition[0,1] at the rho_i, express the integral as a finite positive sum of squared original gradient partial sums, and compare it against the signed diagonalmu. A valid proof would be a weighted discrete inequality on ALL vectors; no independence or freedom of actual histories is used to assert a counterexample if that sufficient test fails.

## 4. Direct relation to the desired complete-H_m upper target

For S=sum H_m, set the desired total weight

    B_target=1+(32/17)S.

For any selected decreasingrho_i in(0,1), the choice

    u_n=B_target-sum_(i<n)rho_i h_i

is positive, because B_target>S. A successful explicitC and fullPSDproof for(5), uniformly over every sufficiently large inheritedm, would give

    W(H_m)<=1/B_target<=17/[16(1+2S)].

The accepted benchmark ratio would then close the original negative direction. No suchrho profile, C array or PSD estimate is given in this note. The mass identity by itself is only bookkeeping.

The positive A/D conic-hull obstruction in the companion note fixed physical function weightsb_i=h_i and imposed nonnegative physical diagonal slack. Hereb_i=rho_i h_i andthe FULLnon-diagonal matrixR is kept. That old obstruction cannot simply be reused without a new derivation. Conversely, a named covariance does not prove the new family feasible.

The R3 weighted-prefix report tested two PARTICULAR outgoing minimizer-row laws for a different last-prefix objective. Its full matrix obstruction must be respected at those exact profiles; it is not a blanket exclusion of allu in(1). The old R2 history family, R3 source laws, and the present original-terminal dominance functional must be matched explicitly before claiming novelty or impossibility.

A future allocation should first choose an actualrho law and matchingC, then prove the full original-space inequality or reject that precise law using an exact original-space obstruction. It should not stop at the equivalent feasibility statement(5), and must not invoke an unallocated solver. No new native, mathematical-program or formal allowance follows from this note.

## Source-method check and a genuine positive-conic-hull exclusion

Primary method sources checked: [Zhao2609.11784v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11784v1), Sections3.1--3.3, and [Chikhi2609.13765v2](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.13765v2), with the constant-step/proximal scope kept separate fromtheoriginalproblem. Theparent manuallygeneralizedZhao'sintervalmatrixmechanism toactualvariableh>=1 andpositiveintervalweightsz_i,v_i.

ForactualtrajectoryBmat withdiagonal1 andlowercolumnh_j, setZ_i=sum_(a..i)z,V_i^+=sum_(i+1..b)v,u_i=h_iZ_i-z_i,w_i=v_i+h_iV_i^+. Off-diagonalintervalentriesC_ij=u_i v_j(i<j),C_ij=w_i z_j(i>j),andzerorowsums giveH=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T diagonal exactly. Multiplierslambda_ij=C_ji are nonnegative.

The NOVELTYCHECKthenidentifiedeachmodeasanoldA/Datom. Morestrongly, theoldcentralcutextends to ANYfinitepositivesum ofsuchatoms. Withq_k=1+2sum_nu A_(k-1)^nu D_k^nu andnonnegativephysicaldiagonalslackmu_k=q_k-(h_k-1)²q_(k+1), combinedcutmatchingb_i=h_i implies

    q_k+sum_(i<k)mu_i=(T_k+1)²-2sum_nu B_k^nu E_k^nu.

At a palindrome'scentralexcessr andchildmasss, everyatommonotonicitygivesq_(k+1)>=(3(s+1)+r)/(s+1-r),while r²q_(k+1)<=q_k<=(s+1)². Hence r/(s+1)<=sqrt2-1. ActualinheritedH_m violatesit. Thisrejectstheentirepositivemodecone BEFORE anyterminalpenalty, notjustoneA/D factorization. No additionalmodel/program was allocated.

SignedmodeexpansionswithnonnegativeFINALentries, otherfunction-valueweights, andnon-diagonalPSDremaindersremainoutside this proof. They are not thereby provedfeasible.

## Unallocated full-objective lead: Brownian-bridge covariance

The finalparentnote changesbothfunctionweightsandthe remainderstructure. Forfixedu_i>=0,totalBcert=sumu_i, the doubledoriginal-radiusdominanceresidual is

    D_u=R0²-sumu_i Q_star,i-2Bcert(f_n-fstar)
         -||x0-xstar-sumu_i g_i||²,

using NONNEGATIVEQ_star,i. Exactexpansiongivesdiagonalu_i(1-u_i) andcrosscoefficientu_i(h_j-u_j),j<i. Choose1>rho0>...>rhon>0,u_i=rho_i h_i fori<n, withu_n>0 separate. Setell_i=h_i(i<n),ell_n=u_n/rho_n (anALGEBRAICweight, notaphysicalstep). The covariance

matchesALLcrosscoefficients andisPSDbytheexactintegralof||sumell_i g_i(1_(t<=rho_i)-rho_i)||² on[0,1]. Thegradientsremainactualdeterministicones.

Forzero-row-sumCwithnonnegativeoffdiagonalentriesandcolumnsb_i=u_i(i<n),b_n=u_n-Bcert,

A completefullPSDproof foractualrho,C withBcert=1+(32/17)S_m wouldclose thefullupper target. NONEisconstructed. EvenwhenH isdiagonal, theFULLR mustbekept; imposingnonnegativeindividualdiagonals discards the proposed mechanism. This is anUNALLOCATEDcandidateinterface, notproofprogressbyitself. # R11 parent supplement: finite left expression and endpoint-normal retention

**UNVERIFIED manual derivation. No all-depth inequality is proved.** Keep all notation and actual sample conditions of R11_PARENT_INPUT_NORMAL_TRANSPORT_NOTE_20261009.md. This supplement does not alter that frozen source's hash or any native grant. No mathematical program was used.

## 1. The left quadratic is independent of the feasible comparison parameter

Suppose the child and proposed parent canonical fractions are the same eta, with chi=1-eta. Write Delta=alpha-b, w0=M p0-A ell, and e=p-r. After inserting the child lower bound EL>=eta RL+gamma BL into the full R10 parent identity, and transporting the input normals as in the earlier note, retain the square chi*M²||p0-ztheta||².

Since

    M(p0-ztheta)=w0+(1-theta)A u+Delta p-(D+sigma_k h)r,
    eN=nR-n0=w0+Au+Delta p-Dr,
    D+sigma_k h-Delta=M-A,

all theta dependence cancels from the resulting finite LEFT expression. With

    R0=w0-(M-A)r=M(p0-r)-A(ell-r),

that expression is exactly

    Q_left=chi||R0||²-chi Delta²||p-r||²
           -2chi Delta sigma_k h<r,p-r>
           -2chi A(M-A)<r,u>.                         (1)

The terms excluded from the label Q_left are: A*gamma*BL; the bridge quadratic Theta||p-r||²; bridge normals with Nr coefficient reduced from hC/(D+k) to hC/(D+k)-chiDelta; the remaining positive input normals

    2chi[A N0(ell)+(M-A)N0(r)];

both signed discount squares; and the complete right-child contribution. Equation(1) must not be treated as a standalone nonnegative quantity. Its final inner product remains substantive.

One way to check (1) is to put Kvec=Delta p-(D+sigma_k h)r. Before completing the square, the relevant terms are

    chi||w0+Kvec||²+2chi A<Kvec,u>
                         -2chi Delta<eN,p-r>.

Expanding eN and using D+sigma_k h-Delta=M-A gives (1). This identity was independently confirmed in a progress message from the R11 upper derivation; no verifier acceptance is implied.

After the actual right-normal payment, the finite right expression for the same eta,gamma is

    Q_right=eta[2kappa hD||uR||²+2D sigma_k h<uR,q>
                       -sigma_k²h²(||q||²-||r||²)]
              +gamma MD||q-r||².                       (2)

The right physical normal rows retain positive coefficients. Equations(1)--(2) expose the full vector debt rather than imposing independent artificial remainder values. A quantitative part of the exact left gap may be needed to control<r,u>.

## 2. The parent endpoint normal can also be retained at the same small fraction

The complete parent endpoint comparison remainder is

    B_all=Rem(x,M,q)=M||p0-q||²+2N0(q).

It has the exact normal transport

    N0(q)=N0(r)+N_Rq+<eN,q-r>,
    N_Rq=<nR,r-q>>=0.                                 (3)

Indeed n0=nR-eN and p0-q=(p0-r)+(r-q). Thus subtracting the SAME endpoint fraction gamma*M*B_all adds the finite terms

    -gamma M²||p0-q||²-2gamma M<eN,q-r>,                (4)

and changes the coefficient of N0(r) to

    2[chi(M-A)-gamma M].                               (5)

The R10 right-normal payment had coefficient2eta h d/D+2gamma M onN_Rq, so its remaining coefficient after(3) is exactly2eta h d/D>=0. No extra loss of the endpoint fraction is needed merely to pay this normal.

For every genuine parent with a nonempty inherited child j>=1, the leaf estimate h_leaf>4/3 gives s>=h_leaf and kappa>=16/17, hence A=1+2kappa s>3. Also h=s+b>s and d>=1 imply M>2A-1. Therefore

    A/M < A/(2A-1) <3/5,
    1-A/M>2/5.

Consequently gamma<=(2/5)chi is a uniform sufficient scalar condition for (5). In particular eta=3/4,gamma=1/1200 satisfies it by a wide margin. The empty-child physical leaf is already covered by the independently derived base and is not silently included in this parent estimate.

This establishes only compatibility of the endpoint NORMAL coefficients. The full quadratic obtained from (1), (2), (4), the child endpoint remainder, the signed discount terms, and all remaining actual projection relations is still unproved. Canonical and endpoint retention at every level do not follow from this calculation alone.

# R11 parent: retain the true initial maximizer to transport the left normal fan

**UNVERIFIED manual derivation. Original target OPEN.** This note changes the R10 allocation by using the individual actual rows inside the parent maximization remainder, rather than discarding that remainder as a nonnegative whole. It does not establish positivity of the remaining quadratic expression or an all-depth U law. No mathematical program or model call produced this note.

Use the exact actual parent notation of research/full_right_gap_u_closure_20261009_r10/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md. One compact convex K contains0; every P denotes its Euclidean projection. Fix kappa in[16/17,1], delta=1-kappa, sigma_k=2kappa-1, d>=1, childmasss, bridgeb=1+k, h=s+b,C=1+s,

    A=1+2kappa s, alpha=A-s, D=d+2kappa s, M=D+2kappa h.

The actual left child is x->v, its gradientspoints areg_i and displacementG=x-v=sum_left h_i g_i. The bridgep=P(v) givesw=v-bp. The right childw->y hasr=P(w/D),q=P(y/d). The left auxiliary projection isell=P(x/A), withnL=x-Aell, zL=(G+alpha p)/A,u=ell-zL.

Now explicitly KEEP the true parent initial maximizer

    p0=P(x/M), n0=x-M p0, w0=nL-n0=M p0-Aell.

All these points and normals are actual samples of the same body. For z inK setN0(z)=<n0,p0-z>>=0. Let

    Fan0=sum_left h_i N0(g_i),
    ztheta=[(1-theta)G+(b-theta alpha)p+(M-h)r+theta Aell]/M,
    Rtheta=Rem(x,M,ztheta).

The guaranteed feasible range remains0<=theta<=min(1,b/alpha). Thus its exact normal part is

    M Rtheta=M²||p0-ztheta||²
       +2[(1-theta)Fan0+(b-theta alpha)N0(p)
           +(M-h)N0(r)+theta A N0(ell)].                  (1)

The old left canonical remainder has RL=A||u||²+2<nL,u>. In the R10 parent identity, after inserting the left-child canonical fraction eta and subtracting the SAME parent fraction eta, the remaining coefficient of<nL,u> is

    -2(1-eta)(1-theta)A<nL,u>.

R10's loss condition arose by treating the normal part of(1) merely as nonnegative. Its rows can instead be used exactly. Putchi=1-eta. SinceG+alpha p=A(ell-u),

    A<nL,u>=Fan0+alpha N0(p)-A N0(ell)+A<w0,u>.      (2)

Therefore the sum of the ENTIRE normal part ofchi*M*Rtheta and this left normal debt is exactly

    2chi[A N0(ell)+(b-alpha)N0(p)+(M-h)N0(r)
                          -(1-theta)A<w0,u>].          (3)

In particular the fan over EVERY original left query cancels, with no shrinking ofeta. This cancellation uses both true initial projections; it is not justified if p0 is replaced by a convenient comparison point.

LetDelta=alpha-b and retain the actual bridge normal

    nR=w-D r, Nr=<nR,r-p>>=0,
    eN=nR-n0=w0+Au+Delta p-D r.

The exact relationN0(p)-N0(r)=Nr-<eN,r-p> turns(3) into

    2chi[A N0(ell)+(M-A)N0(r)-Delta Nr
           +Delta<eN,r-p>-(1-theta)A<w0,u>].           (4)

HereM-A=d-1+2kappa h>0. Thus the only possibly negative normal coefficient newly exposed by this transport is the root coefficient ofNr. All other new terms outside normal cones are explicit finite-vector quadratic expressions.

In the full parent identity the bridge rows originally have coefficients

    2Kp Np+2Kr0 Nr,
    Np=<v-p,p-r>>=0,
    Kp=h(1+C/(D+k)), Kr0=hC/(D+k).

After(4), the new Nr coefficient is2Kr, with

    Kr=hC/(D+k)-chi Delta.

It is NOT uniformly nonnegative for alld: whenDelta>0 andeta<1, the first term tends0 asd grows. Do not claim that the root coefficient always pays it directly.

There is nevertheless an exact all-d transport of this remaining normal. Let e=p-r. The two ACTUAL bridge normals obey

    Np+Nr=<k p+D r,p-r>=(D+k)<p,e>-D||e||².         (5)

IfKr<0, useNr=(Np+Nr)-Np. Then

    2Kp Np+2Kr Nr
      =2(h+chi Delta)Np
          +2Kr[(D+k)<p,e>-D||e||²].                (6)

The coefficienth+chi Delta is positive: ifDelta>=0 this is immediate, while ifDelta<0, h+chiDelta>=h+Delta=s+alpha>0. IfKr>=0, both original remaining bridge normal coefficients are already nonnegative and may instead be retained. Thus no unbounded left-fan or root-normal debt needs to be left as an independent scalar. The price is explicit quadratic terms involving p0,ell,u,p,r; their sign has NOT been established.

This calculation does not consume the squarechi*M²||p0-ztheta||² in(1), the positive retained child squares, the exact right-child gap, or the nonnegative normals in(4). They must be included when testing a complete same-fraction or richer induction. A coefficient matrix with freely varying vectors is only a sufficient screen; a negative direction does not itself give a realizable counterexample.

The previous R10 right-normal payment remains available with its exact actual convex weights. Any parent retention of the endpoint comparison remainder B needs its OWN transport and cannot be assumed from the canonical remainder calculation above. Similarlyeta3/4 andgamma1/1200 are only a proved-in-draft leaf base, not a higher-level assertion.

The concrete next proof obligation is to combine(1)--(6) with the complete R10 parent identity, preserve the actual radial/bridge/right-child projection relations, and prove a recursively sufficient bound at finalkappa>=16/17. The cancellation removes the specific expanding-normal-loss argument; it does not certify the full inequality or the original minimax theorem.

# Unallocated R11 source lead: positive interval modes for a variable physical word

**UNVERIFIED manual derivation; the positive interval dictionary is REFUTED at its stated certificate scope by Section5. No model/program allocation or target certificate.** The source check was for proof methods, not a posted solution of the exact original benchmark. The original Goal remains OPEN. No new mathematical execution was used.

[Jinze Zhao, Constant Steps Are s-Composable, arXiv:2609.11784v1](https://arxiv.org/html/2609.11784v1), Sections3.1--3.3, constructs positive interval-supported interpolation matrices whose product with a constant-step trajectory matrix has diagonal symmetric part. Its theorem concerns a specially balanced CONSTANT schedule; it does not establish a variable-word or perturbed-Silver theorem. The identities below are a manual variable-step extension of that matrix mechanism, not an import of its positivity theorem for combined weights.

## 1. Exact matrix mode for an arbitrary positive word

Let the physical word be h_0,...,h_(n-1), with h_i>=1 for this mode construction. Let B be the (n+1)-square trajectory matrix

    B_ij=h_j for j<i, B_ii=1, B_ij=0 for j>i.

Fix a contiguous interval [a,b] of query indices, a<b. Choose positive weights z_i,v_i for i in[a,b], and set

    Z_i=sum_(a<=k<=i) z_k,  Z_i^-=sum_(a<=k<i) z_k,
    V_i^+=sum_(i<k<=b) v_k, V_i=v_i+V_i^+,
    u_i=h_i Z_i-z_i, w_i=v_i+h_i V_i^+.

The value h_b is irrelevant wherever b is the terminal query: u_b has no upper entries and w_b=v_b. It can be given any dummy value without changing the matrix. Define

    C_ij=u_i v_j for a<=i<j<=b,
    C_ij=w_i z_j for a<=j<i<=b,
    C_ii=-sum_(j!=i) C_ij,

with entries outside the interval zero. Every off-diagonal entry is nonnegative, because u_i=h_i Z_i^-+(h_i-1)z_i>=0 and w_i>0. Every row sums tozero.

For i<j in the interval,

    (CB)_ij=u_i(v_j+h_j V_j^+)=u_i w_j,
    (CB)_ji=w_j(z_i-h_i Z_i)=-w_j u_i.

The second equality uses the zero row sum. Entries of CB outside the interval vanish: earlier columns are h_j times a zero row sum, and later columns are zero by triangularity. Therefore H=CB+B^T C^T is DIAGONAL, with

    H_ii=2[(h_i-1)^2 Z_i V_i^+ - Z_i^- V_i].        (1)

For i=b this is -2 Z_b^- v_b, independent of the dummy h_b. To check(1),

    (CB)_ii=(h_i-1)u_i V_i^+ - w_i Z_i^-
            =(h_i-1)^2 Z_i V_i^+ - Z_i^- V_i.

With constant h and z=v=1 this reproduces the source's interval mode. For variable h it keeps the ACTUAL physical trajectory matrix, not a reparameterized clock. No smoothness or interpolation condition is changed.

## 2. Exact smooth-convex inequality represented by the mode

Let bvec denote the column-sum vector of C, so sum bvec=0. For actual normalized smooth-convex data define

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||²>=0.

Then lambda_ij=C_ji>=0 for i!=j, and the exact identity is

    sum_(i,j) C_ji Q_ij
       =2 sum_i bvec_i f_i + sum_i(H_ii-bvec_i)||g_i||².   (2)

Indeed beta_i=(Bg)_i=g_i+x0-x_i gives

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j+2<g_j,beta_i-beta_j>-||g_i||²+||g_j||².

Summing the full zero-row-sum C, including its harmless diagonal sinceQ_ii=0, eliminates the beta_j terms and leaves2g^T C B g. Equation(1) makes this diagonal. This also fixes the transpose convention; using C_ij rather thanC_ji as the interpolation multiplier would give a different expression.

Positive sums of these modes remain valid. However, no mode weights have been constructed that match the actual original-target objective on H_m. Nonnegativity of EACH mode does not imply nonnegativity of the coefficients needed to combine them.

## 3. A concrete sufficient whole-word target, not a claimed solution

For mass S=sum h and kappa=16/17, let

    sigma=2kappa-1=15/17, tau=1+sigma S,
    B_terminal=tau(tau-1)=sigma S(1+sigma S).

The actual smooth-convex history expression

    E_history=2 sum_(i<n) h_i(f_i-f_n)
              -sum_(i<n)h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||²
              -B_terminal||g_n||²                         (3)

would be nonnegative if an explicit nonnegative combination of modes yielded column sums

    bvec_i=h_i for i<n, bvec_n=-S,

and diagonal entries satisfying

    H_ii=h_i(2-h_i)-mu_i for i<n,
    H_nn=-S-B_terminal-mu_n,
    mu_i>=0 for EVERY index.

Then(2) plus sum mu_i||g_i||² equals(3). This is a checkable sufficient coefficient target with real physical steps. It is NOT a solved coefficient problem or a necessity theorem.

For a selected original minimizer, (3) plus sum_(i<n)h_i Q_star,i gives

    ||xn-xstar||²+2S(f_n-fstar)+B_terminal||g_n||²
          <=||x0-xstar||².

Equivalently, u_i=h_i for i<n andu_n=tau give a Zhang--Jiang dominance residual, with total weight

    sum u_i=S+tau=1+2kappa S.

To see this directly, use Q_star,i=2fstar-2f_i+2<g_i,x_i-xstar>-||g_i||² in the NONNEGATIVE interpolation convention above. The doubled dominance residual is

    ||x0-xstar||²-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2(sum u_i)(f_n-fstar)-||x0-xstar-sum_i u_i g_i||².

Its exact expansion equals(3), because every cross-gradient coefficientu_i(h_j-u_j),j<i, vanishes. The minus sign in front ofsum u_i Q_star,i is essential with this nonnegative convention. Nonnegativity of(3) would thus imply the full-class value bound

    W_n(h)<=1/[1+(32/17)S] <=17/[16(1+2S)].

No such nonnegative combination for the complete inheritedH_m is supplied here. The positive-mode dictionary and the exact coefficient target must not be described as an upper certificate.

## 4. Required novelty and scope checks before any new allocation

The old R2 dense A/D diagonal-history family, even with arbitrary nonnegative diagonal slack and terminal penalty, was excluded on its stated scope. This mode dictionary must be compared with that actual family before being treated as a new route; no claim of strict containment or escape is made. The R7 all-level q_j terminal potential was actually false, so a reduced coefficient cannot simply be propagated by the primitive join theorem. The uniform B_terminal here is a distinct sufficient candidate, not a theorem or an approved repair.

A useful continuation would have to give actual nonnegative interval coefficients/slack on ALL relevant physical words, or show a sharp obstruction to this precise dictionary. Returning only the linear feasibility conditions above repeats an unproved certificate problem. This note carries NO new model, mathematical-program or formal allowance. The source's constant-step theorem, the Moreau/proximal objective criterion, and its balanced rate identity do not resolve the original Zhang--Jiang profile target.

## 5. Completed novelty check: the conic hull still has the central-bridge obstruction

After deriving Sections1--4, the parent read COMPLETE Sections2--4 of research/dense_history_search_20261008_r2/REPORT.md and its ROOT_DERIVATION_UNVERIFIED.md. Each interval mode above is an atom of that older A/D family. Specifically, extend

    A_k=sum_(a<=i<=min(k,b)) z_i, withA_k=0 beforea,
    D_k=sum_(max(k,a)<=i<=b) v_i, withD_k=0 afterb.

Then, for i<j,

    C_ji=(A_i-A_(i-1))(D_j+(h_j-1)D_(j+1)),
    C_ij=(A_(i-1)+(h_i-1)A_i)(D_j-D_(j+1)).        (4)

The terminal h_n is bookkeeping only; its term multipliesD_(n+1)=0. Weak monotonicity, including A_0=0 for intervals that start later, is enough. Formula(4) matches every original off-diagonal entry, not merely a terminal scalar.

A positive SUM of modes need not itself factor into one pairA,D. However the old central-cut proof extends to its entire finite positive conic hull. This requires a separate argument, given here rather than assuming that one-atom exclusion implies sum exclusion.

Index the atoms bynu and absorb their nonnegative combination weights intoA^nu. Define

    Z_k=sum_nu A_k^nu D_(k+1)^nu,
    q_k=1+2sum_nu A_(k-1)^nu D_k^nu,
    T_k=sum_(i<k)h_i,
    B_k^nu=sum_(i<k)h_i A_i^nu,
    E_k^nu=sum_(i>=k, i<n)h_i D_(i+1)^nu.

If the combined function coefficients are bvec_i=h_i fori<n andbvec_n=-S, the cut equations are exactly

    sum_nu[A_k^nu E_k^nu-D_(k+1)^nu B_k^nu
                         -2(h_k-1)A_k^nu D_(k+1)^nu]
       =T_k+h_k.                                       (5)

The physical diagonal residuals are

    mu_k=q_k-(h_k-1)^2 q_(k+1).

The same increment calculation as in the older prefix identity, now summing each bilinear atom product, proves

    q_k+sum_(i<k)mu_i
       =(T_k+1)^2-2sum_nu B_k^nu E_k^nu.               (6)

For clarity, B_(k+1)^nu=B_k^nu+h_k A_k^nu and E_(k+1)^nu=E_k^nu-h_k D_(k+1)^nu. The increment of the right side of(6), using(5), is[1-(h_k-1)^2]q_(k+1), exactly the increment on the left. Both sides start at1. Thus(6) needs only the COMBINED cut equations, not the false assumption that each atom separately matches them.

Since each B_k^nu E_k^nu>=0, nonnegative physical residuals implyq_k<=(T_k+1)^2. Now consider a palindrome with equal left/right masses s and middle physicalstep1+r, r>0, atindexk. SetC=s+1. Monotonicity givesE_(k+1)^nu<=s D_(k+1)^nu in EACH atom. The central cut(5), with the bridge separated from its right suffix, therefore gives

    C+r=sum_nu[A_k^nu E_(k+1)^nu+(1-r)A_k^nuD_(k+1)^nu
                                -D_(k+1)^nu B_k^nu]
         <=(C-r) Z_k.                                 (7)

The left side is positive, so C>r and Z_k>0. Hence

    q_(k+1)=1+2Z_k >=(3C+r)/(C-r).

At the same physical bridge, mu_k>=0 and(6) imply

    r² q_(k+1)<=q_k<=C².

With x=r/C in(0,1),

    x²(3+x)/(1-x)<=1,
    (x+1)(x²+2x-1)<=0,
    r/(s+1)<=sqrt2-1.                                 (8)

The weak inequality is sufficient. No strict-positivity assumption on every atom at every query was used.

The actual inherited central bridge violates(8). Withc0=1+sqrt2,epsilon=1/(4m),beta=1-epsilon,c=c0(1+epsilon),a=beta/c andj=m-1,

    r/(s+1)=(c-2)/[1+tau(2/c)^j],
    tau=[c(c-2)-beta]/beta,
    0<tau<9epsilon.

Forj>=3, (1/c0)*tau*(2/c)^j<72epsilon/c0^4<c0epsilon becausec0^5=41+29sqrt2>72. Sincec-2=1/c0+c0epsilon, this givesr/(s+1)>1/c0. Allm>=1536 havej>=3. Thus the entire positive conic hull of the variable-step interval modes is infeasible for the desired physical coefficient/diagonal-sign target, BEFORE imposing any terminal penalty.

This is a complete manual obstruction to the stated dictionary, not a full-PEP impossibility and not a counterexample to(3). It includes arbitrary finite mode sets, arbitrary positive z,v within intervals, arbitrary nonnegative combination weights, and arbitrary nonnegative physical diagonal slack. It does not include a signed mode expansion whose FINAL interpolation entries remain nonnegative, arbitrary nonseparable matrices not in this cone, non-diagonal PSD remainders, or different function-value weights.

No new model or mathematical program should be allocated to retuning POSITIVE weights in this dictionary. The source-derived algebra is valid, but its positive-sum route repeats the old central obstruction in a larger, now explicitly treated scope.

# R12 original calls complete: fixed A/B rejected, Profile C open

All physical queries are included; only the last physical query has gradient magnitude beta rather than1. EveryQ_star,i=0; the ORIGINALradius isX_m².

Forr=sqrt2-1,v=r²/(1+2sqrt2), the literal enclosing-m limits are

The source proofs provide exact finite-depth formulas and uniform geometric error bounds; no fixed-internal-depth limit is substituted. Profile A satisfies

ThusD_u=2X_m Z-Z²-Bcert t²<0. For any nonnegative original interpolation coefficientsC_ji with required row/column conditions,

The same ACTUAL gradient vector excludes FULLPSD for everyC; no diagonal restriction, old A/D ansatz, or free-vector screen is involved. The two independent derivations agree; that agreement is not Danus verifier acceptance.

# Unallocated next lead: excess physical weights and a Laplacian remainder

**UNVERIFIED manual algebra; no complete certificate or new allocation.** The original Goal remains OPEN. This note defines a concrete alternative to the decreasing-clock profiles. It does not declare Profile C false or solve the original target.

## 1. A source-grounded motivation, with its exact boundary

In the canonical primitive--dominant concatenation of [Zhang--Jiang, Section3.2](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2), the left physical weights are the left steps, the bridge weight isgamma=s_left+2, and the right dominant vector is multiplied bytau>1. The physical bridge isbeta=1+(s_left+1)/tau<=gamma. Consequently the canonical recursively constructed dominant vector satisfiesu_i>=h_i at every PHYSICAL index. This is a property of those source-constructed schedules and weights; it does not make the actual perturbedH_m dominant.

The failed A/B profiles instead reduced physical weights and assigned the missing total to the terminal gradient. Their actual counterexample shows why that terminal residual square can be too large. The following fixed candidate puts more mass on physical gradients and less at the terminal query.

## 2. Explicit increasing-clock candidate

For the literal inheritedH_m letS=sumh,T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t andBcert=1+(32/17)S. Set

    u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i, i<n,
    u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.                          (1)

The physical weights satisfyu_i>=h_i. WithV=sum h_i²,

    sum_(i<n)u_i=5S/4-V/(4S),
    u_n>=1+(43/68)S>0.

The strict inequality usesV>=0; no additional physical step is introduced. The desired total remainsBcert, distinct from the original benchmarkB_n.

Use NONNEGATIVEQ_star,i and define the original doubled dominance functional

    D_u=||x0-xstar||²-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2Bcert(f_n-fstar)-||x0-xstar-sum_i u_i g_i||².

For any nonnegativeu, its exact gradient expansion is

    D_u=2sum_(i<n)u_i(f_i-f_n)
      +sum_i u_i(1-u_i)||g_i||²
      +2sum_(i<j)u_j(h_i-u_i)<g_i,g_j>.              (2)

Every h_i in the last sum is physical, becausei<j<=n.

## 3. Exact positive gradient-difference energy

Fori<j definew_ij=u_j(u_i-h_i)>=0. LetL be the graph Laplacian with off-diagonalL_ij=-w_ij and diagonalL_ii=sum_(j!=i)w_(min(i,j),max(i,j)). Then

    g^T L g=sum_(i<j) w_ij||g_i-g_j||²>=0.

The off-diagonal coefficients of(2) are EXACTLY those ofL. For any zero-row-sum interpolation matrixC with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and column sumsb_i=u_i(i<n),b_n=u_n-Bcert, putH=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T using the ACTUAL trajectory matrix. The exact full remainder is

    R=L+diag(u_i(1-u_i)+b_i-L_ii)-H,
    D_u=sum_(i,j)C_ji Q_ij+g^T R g.                  (3)

This is a deterministic original-gradient identity. IfH is diagonal, the remainder is a positive gradient-difference energy plus a signed diagonal; nonnegative individual diagonal entries would be sufficient but are not assumed necessary. IfH is not diagonal, its whole contribution must be retained. NeitherL>=0 nor the mass identity in(1) provesR>=0.

Sinceu_i-[h_i,0]_i is componentwise nonnegative, the residual vector can also be written

    x0-sum_i u_i g_i=x_n-sum_i(u_i-[h_i,0]_i)g_i.

For projection envelopes, the latter excess-weighted gradient divided by its totalBcert-S is a genuine convex combination in the SAMEbody. This may provide a history-dependent comparison point. It is not an actual extra query or permission to change the function.

## 4. Exact critical-orbit calibration, not a validity claim

The complete symmetric-Huber orbit in the R12 A/B counterexample hasr=sqrt2-1,v=r²/(1+2sqrt2), X_m/S->r andM_m/S²->v, whereM_m=sum h_i x_(i+1). Generalizing(1) to u_i=(1+theta*T_i/S)h_i withtheta>0, finite summation by parts gives

    U_phys=S+theta*(S²-V)/(2S),
    G_phys=X_m-(1+theta)x_n+theta*M_m/S.

At odd depths, whereg_n=x_n->-r, the totalZ=sumu_i g_i satisfies

    Z/S ->2r/17+theta*(3sqrt2-4).

At even depths,

    Z/S ->r(1+15/17)-theta*(5sqrt2-6)/7.

For the fixedtheta1/2 in(1), both limits lie strictly between0 and2r. SinceBcert*f_n/S²->0, the leading dominance residual on this ONEorbit is positive at both parities. This proves only that the known A/B counterexample does not reject the new candidate. Other functions, starts and dimensions have not been checked.

## 5. Actual remaining proof obligation

A continuation must choose explicitC and prove the FULLmatrixR in(3) PSD, uniformly over every sufficiently large inheritedm, or produce a different actual complete-wordD_u<0 witness. A genuine fullPSDproof would implyW(H_m)<=1/Bcert and close the conditional original-target separation through accepted931b9653ce0f7201. No such proof exists in this note.

The acyclic-flow diagonal obstruction still applies whenU_phys=O(S) andthe centralu_i is orderS. The single nonnegative destination-law obstruction in R12_PARENT_RANKONE_FLOW_OBSTRUCTION_20261009.md also applies because every fixed earlyu_i tends to its step andU_phys->infinity. Thus simple causal transport or retuning one global destination distribution cannot establish this candidate. A usefulC needs genuinely index-dependent circulations or another explicit original-space construction.

The old positive-interval-cone result fixed b_i=h_i and separately nonnegative physical diagonal slack; it cannot be transferred automatically to(3). Conversely, merely renaming its feasibility system using a Laplacian is not progress. The candidate has NOmodel, mathematical-program or formal allowance attached.

## Fixed A/B profiles: complete actual all-depth rejection

ReadR12_AB_COUNTEREXAMPLE_HANDOFF_20261009.md and its two complete source proofs. WithS=sumH_m,T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t,Bcert=1+(32/17)S, the fixed profiles were

    X1=beta, X_j=S_(j-1)+1+k_(j-1)-X_(j-1), x0=X_m.

## New unallocated source-grounded lead

# R12 concrete dominance profiles for the complete inherited word

**UNVERIFIED candidate functionals, not upper certificates.** These are fixed mathematical objects before the two R12 searches. No claim is made that their dominance residual is nonnegative. The original Goal remains OPEN.

For one enclosing m>=1536 keep epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon, c=(1+sqrt2)(1+epsilon), a=beta/c, H0empty, H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a*c^j,H_j). Let n=2^m-1, h=H_m, S=sum h, T_i=sum_(t<i)h_t, T_0=0,T_n=S. Use the SAME enclosing parameters in all children.

Let Bcert=1+(32/17)S. This is a proposed certificate weight, NOT the exact original benchmark B_n. The two profiles are:

    Profile A: rho_i=1-T_i/(2S), 0<=i<=n.
    Profile B: rho_i=(S+1)/(S+1+T_i), 0<=i<=n.

For either profile, set u_i=rho_i h_i fori<n and

    u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i>0.

Indeed0<rho_i<=1 andBcert>S. Every weight is fixed by the entire physical word before a function is chosen. In Profile A, rho_0=1,rho_n=1/2, and the exact identity

    sum_(i<n)u_i=3S/4+sum_(i<n)h_i²/(4S)

follows from2sum h_i T_i=S²-sum h_i². The covariance is degenerate at the initial node because rho_0=1; a proof must handle that actual boundary, not replace it silently by a strict inequality. All other rho_i decrease strictly because the physical H_m steps are positive.

For any normalized convex1-smooth function with original selected minimizer x_star and actual GD path, define NONNEGATIVE interpolation rows

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||²,
    Q_star,i=2fstar-2f_i+2<g_i,x_i-xstar>-||g_i||².

The exact doubled dominance residual for either profile is

    D_u=||x0-xstar||²-sum_(i=0)^n u_i Q_star,i
        -2Bcert(f_n-fstar)-||x0-xstar-sum_(i=0)^n u_i g_i||².

It has no free function-dependent weights. A full-class proofD_u>=0 for every original trajectory would implyW(H_m)<=1/Bcert<=17/(16D_m), whereD_m=1+2S. Accepted931b9653ce0f7201 would then give the desired strict asymptotic separation. Conversely, a legal trajectory withD_u<0 rejects that profile, not the full upper bound or the original minimax proposition.

For the deterministic covariance representation putell_i=h_i(i<n),ell_n=u_n/rho_n; ell_n is NOT a physical update. Define

    K_ij=ell_i ell_j[min(rho_i,rho_j)-rho_i rho_j].

It is PSD by the squared-integral identity in R11_BROWNIAN_BRIDGE_DOMINANCE_LEAD_20261009.md. With the ACTUAL trajectory matrixBmat_ij=h_j(j<i),Bmat_ii=1, choose zero-row-sumC with nonnegative off-diagonal entries and column sums

    b_i=u_i(i<n), b_n=-sum_(i<n)u_i=u_n-Bcert.

Then the exact full remainder is

    R=K+diag(u_i(1-ell_i)+b_i)-(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T),
    D_u=sum_(i,j)C_ji Q_ij+g^T R g.

The concrete upper task is to constructC and proveR PSD for ALL sufficiently large inheritedm. The covariance alone is not enough. IfH=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T is diagonal, signed entries ofR-K are allowed; replacing fullPSD by their separate nonnegativity would discard the new mechanism.

These profiles differ from the R3 last-prefix laws and from physical-weight positive-diagonal certificates. Their exact relation to any old obstruction must be checked, not guessed. No positive interval-mode retuning under the old b_i=h_i/nonnegative-diagonal assumptions is revived.

The independent primal search concerns these already fixed D_u functionals, not an unfinished C object from another search. It must produce actual fixed functions, actual trajectories, the original selected minimizer/radius, and a strict sign on an unbounded sequence of allowed enclosingm to reject an asymptotic profile. A finite-horizon toy or a freely assigned gradient table is not such a rejection.

# Parent R12 extension: every single nonnegative destination law fails

**UNVERIFIED manual proof. This excludes a coefficient family, not Profile C or the original minimax problem.** No new model or mathematical program was used. The R12b report checked one explicit rank-one flow; this note proves the stated larger single-law scope without changing that report's bytes.

Let the physical source weights be u_i>0 fori<n and letU=sum_(i<n)u_i. The required column sums areb_i=u_i physically andb_n=-U. Consider ANY matrix

    C=p q^T-diag(p), p_i>=0,q_i>=0,sum q_i=1,

whose column sums areb. WriteP=sum p_i. Then

    p_i=P q_i-u_i (i<n), p_n=P q_n+U,
    P>=U.

There is no restriction to q_i proportional to the algebraicell_i. Both p andq may depend arbitrarily on the whole word.

For a physical nodei letA_i=sum_(j<i)q_j andF_i=sum_(j>i)q_j. The FULL dominance remainder has diagonal

    R_ii=u_i(2-u_i)-2p_i[(h_i-1)F_i-A_i].          (1)

This follows from the actual lower-triangular trajectory matrix and already includes the complete covariance or other exact decomposition of the dominance cross terms.

Suppose along a sequence of growing words thatU->infinity, that there is a FIXED physical indexJ, and that for0<=i<=J:

- theu_i remain bounded;
- h_i-1 is bounded below by one positive constant;
- u_J>2 by one fixed positive margin for all sufficiently large words.

Then no such single-lawC can haveR PSD for all sufficiently large words. Proof follows.

AssumeR_ii>=0 for every0<=i<=J. Inductively prove thatp_i remains bounded for each such fixedi, or obtain a contradiction already at that node. Ifp_j is bounded forj<i, then

    P A_i=sum_(j<i)(p_j+u_j)=O(1),

andP>=U->infinity. The physical weight strictly afteri isU_after=U-sum_(j<=i)u_j, which tends to infinity. Nonnegativity ofp_j gives

    P F_i>=U_after.

Ifq_i>=1/2 along an unbounded subsequence, thenp_i=Pq_i-u_i>=P/2-O(1), whilep_i<=P. Hence

    2p_i[(h_i-1)F_i-A_i]
      >=(1-o(1)) r_min U_after-O(1) ->infinity.

This contradicts(1), becauseu_i(2-u_i) is bounded above. Thusq_i<1/2 eventually. SinceA_i=O(1/P), nowF_i=1-A_i-q_i>=1/2-o(1), and

    (h_i-1)F_i-A_i>=r_min/3>0

for all sufficiently large words. Equation(1) forcesp_i to be bounded whenu_i(2-u_i)>=0; if that latter number is negative, it already gives a contradiction. This proves the induction. AtindexJ, u_J>2 makesu_J(2-u_J)<0, so the contradiction necessarily occurs byJ.

For Profile C on the literal inheritedH_m, U>=7S_m/8->infinity. At each fixed early indexrho_i->1, henceu_i->h_i^0. The first four physical steps converge to

    sqrt2, 2, sqrt2, 1+(1+sqrt2).

Thus J=3 satisfies every hypothesis, includingu_3->2+sqrt2>2. The entire nonnegative single-destination-law class is therefore excluded at sufficiently large enclosingm, even ifq is retuned as a function ofm. The same argument applies to other clock profiles with these explicitly stated early-weight and total-source limits.

This does not exclude sums with different destination laws, general state-dependent circulations, arbitrary nonseparableC, or the possibility thatD_u>=0 has a different certificate. No negative direction here is asserted to be a realizable function trajectory. The result is only a necessary full-matrix diagnostic for the named coefficient family.

# R12 explicit second-approach modification: smaller linear decay

**UNVERIFIED candidate, not a certificate.** Profiles A/B in the original fixed-profile document are unchanged and have an actual all-depth counterexample under development, independently matched by both R12 derivations. This addendum defines a NEW explicit Profile C for use only if an existing R12 invocation has its second analytical approach unused. It creates no extra invocation, approach, mathematical-program or formal allowance.

Keep the literal same-enclosing-m H_m, physical massS, prefixT_i and desired totalBcert=1+(32/17)S. For a parameter theta in(0,1), let

    rho_i=1-theta*T_i/S, 0<=i<=n,
    u_i=rho_i h_i fori<n,
    u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.

Profile C fixes theta=1/8. Thusrho_0=1,rho_n=7/8; the initial covariance boundary remains explicit. All these weights are predetermined by the whole word. The original doubled dominance functionalD_u and full matrixR are exactly those in R12_FIXED_DOMINANCE_PROFILES_20261009.md. The terminalalgebraic weightell_n=u_n/rho_n is not another physical update.

## Actual critical Huber test and the exact parameter threshold

Use the ONE symmetric Huber functionF(x)=x²/2 for|x|<=1 and|x|-1/2 otherwise, with original minimum0. Letk_j=a c^j,s_j=sumH_j and

    X_1=beta,
    X_j=s_(j-1)+1+k_(j-1)-X_(j-1), j>=2.

The R12 native calculations provek_j>=X_j, so the literal H_j started atX_j has a saturated firstchild/bridge and reflects to-X_(j-1). Its terminal gradient is(-1)^j a beta. All Q_star,i vanish on this projection-envelope trajectory. No radius is reset: x0=X_m.

WriteV_m=sum physicalh_i², M_m=sum_(i<n)h_i x_(i+1), and put

    r=sqrt2-1, v=r²/(1+2sqrt2).

Both R12 derivations have independently obtained, on the literal epsilon=1/(4m) family,

    X_m/S ->r, V_m/S²->v, M_m/S²->v.

These are draft native results pending the parent's final complete read; the algebra below is conditional on those exact trajectory/asymptotic statements. It is not an independent proof of them.

For ANYtheta, summation by parts gives the exact physical sums

    U_phys=sum_(i<n)u_i=S-theta*(S²-V_m)/(2S),
    G_phys=sum_(i<n)u_i g_i
          =X_m-(1-theta)x_n-theta*M_m/S.

On oddm, letZ=sum_(i=0)^n u_i g_i=G_phys+u_n g_n. Its limiting normalized value is

    Z/S ->r(1-15/17)-theta*[v+r(1-v)/2]
         =2r/17-theta*(3sqrt2-4).                     (1)

The last identity usesv=(8sqrt2-11)/7. This same actual orbit therefore rejects everyfixedtheta>(2+sqrt2)/17: thenZ<0 for all sufficiently large oddm, and

    D_u=2X_m Z-Z²-Bcert*(a beta)²<0.

Profile A hadtheta1/2 and lies in this rejected range. This is a necessary test on an actual full trajectory, not a free-gradient matrix screen.

For Profile C, the right side of(1) equals

    (52-35sqrt2)/136 >1/68>0,

using sqrt2<10/7. It is also less than2r. For evenm the analogous asymptotic sum is

    Z/S ->r(1+15/17)+theta*[r(1-v)/2-v].

At theta1/8 its distance below2r is(27sqrt2-10)/952>0. Thus this particular critical Huber orbit does NOT reject Profile C at either parity for sufficiently largem. This is not evidence of validity on other actual functions, other starts, or all dimensions.

The next mathematical task is an actual matrixC and proof of the FULL original-spaceR>=0, or a different legal whole-hierarchy trajectory that makesD_u<0. Passing(1) cannot be substituted for that task. No claim is made that Profile C achieves the original benchmark, and no complete target-level candidate exists.

# Unallocated parent lead: a quantitatively relaxed primitive--dominant join

**UNVERIFIED manual derivation. Original target OPEN.** This is a complete conditional joining calculation with a proved scalar PSD margin. The required all-depth left history inequality is NOT established. No new model/program/formal allocation is attached. The calculation adapts the retained interpolation mechanism of [Zhang--Jiang, Section3.2](https://arxiv.org/html/2410.12395v2), keeping its actual hypotheses explicit.

## 1. Exact full-class input certificates

Normalize L=1 and keep one convex1-smooth function with an ORIGINAL selected minimizer. Translate that minimizer to0 and its value to0 for notation only. Use NONNEGATIVE rows

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||²,
    Q_star,i=-2f_i+2<g_i,x_i>-||g_i||².

A left physical word of masss runs x->v, p=grad f(v). Its retained weak primitive input is

    E_L=||x||²-sum_left h_i Q_star,i
            -2s f(v)-||v||²-Gamma||p||²>=0.          (1)

Equivalently this is the actual history expression

    E_L=2sum_left h_i(f_i-f(v))
          -sum_left h_i(h_i-1)||g_i||²-Gamma||p||².

After the physical bridgeb=1+k, w=v-bp, a right physical word runsw->y. Let fixed nonnegative right weights have totalB_R>0 and weighted gradientU=sum_right u_i g_i, including the actual terminalquery. Its retained weak dominance input is

    D_R=||w||²-sum_right u_i Q_star,i-2B_R f(y)
                                -||w-U||²>=0.       (2)

The right physical mass need not equal(B_R-1)/2. Both(1) and(2) must actually hold for all histories on their specified words. They cannot be inferred from scalar capacities alone.

Choosegamma>=0,tau>=0 and parent sourcevector

    u_parent=(h_left, gamma, tau*u_right),
    B_P=s+gamma+tau B_R.

Every weight remains attached to an actual physical or terminal query.

## 2. The exact retained joining identity

LetA_Q=sum_right u_i Q_(v,i)>=0, wherev denotes the actual bridge query. The row identity

    Q_(v,i)=Q_star,i+2f(v)-||p||²+2<g_i,p-v>

and(2) give EXACTLY

    2B_R[f(v)-f(y)]
       =B_R||p||²+2k<p,U>+||U||²+D_R+A_Q.          (3)

The doubled parent dominance residual expands to

    D_P=E_L+[tau+(s+gamma)/B_R]D_R
         +[(s+gamma)/B_R]A_Q
         +Aq||p||²+2Bq<p,U>+Cq||U||²,              (4)

where

    Aq=Gamma+s+2gamma-gamma²,
    Bq=(s+gamma)k/B_R+tau(b-gamma),
    Cq=(s+gamma)/B_R-tau(tau-1).                    (5)

To check(4), first use(1) to expose the left residual and(2) to obtain

    D_P=E_L+tau D_R+2(s+gamma)[f(v)-f(y)]
          +(Gamma+gamma-gamma²)||p||²
          +2tau(b-gamma)<p,U>-tau(tau-1)||U||².

Substitution of(3) gives(4). Thus if the two-by-two scalar matrix with entriesAq,Bq,Cq is PSD, the original parent dominance residual is nonnegative. The ACTUAL child residuals and bridge-to-right interpolation rows have positive coefficients; they are not silently replaced by primitive positivity at higher levels.

This is a full smooth-convex statement, not just the projection subclass at selected0. Translating the original minimum back restores its original radius without changing any physical step.

## 3. A uniform PSD margin on the actual inherited hierarchy

Take the actual parentH_(j+1) withj>=1 andsame enclosingm>=1536. Its equal childmass iss=S_j, capacityC=1+s, bridgeexcessk=a c^j andratio r=k/C. Elementary inherited mass bounds give

    C>7/3, 2/5<r<5/12.                              (6)

For the lower ratio useC<=c^j and a>2/5; the latter follows fromepsilon<=1/6144 andc0<17/7. For the upper usek/C<=c-2<5/12. These are actual finite-parameter bounds, not limits.

Setkappa=16/17, useB_R=1+2kappa s, and prescribeB_P=1+2kappa(2s+b). Choose

    gamma=1+(3/4)C,
    tau=[B_P-s-gamma]/B_R,
    Gamma >=(7/10)C²-C.                             (7)

DefineD0=4(32-15/C) andN=9+128r. Then

    tau=1+N/D0>1,
    Aq/C²>=11/80,
    Cq=(119-N)/D0-N²/D0²,
    Bq/C=r-3/4+(128r²+32r-27/4)/D0.                (8)

Here716/7<D0<128. Cq decreases withr. At its worst r=5/12 it is increasing inD0 throughout this interval, since2(187/3)²-(170/3)*128=4658/9>0. Consequently

    Cq >842639/4613904 >9/50.                       (9)

The last comparison is the positive integer inequality42131950>41525136. The expressionBq/C increases withr and decreases withD0 on(6), giving

    -1827/12800 < Bq/C < -1333/25776 <0,
    |Bq|<C/7.                                      (10)

Indeed1827*7=12789<12800. Therefore

    Aq*Cq-Bq² >[(11/80)(9/50)-1/49]C²
                  =(851/196000)C²>0.               (11)

Both diagonal entries are positive. Thus the joining matrix is uniformly positive definite. In additiongamma>=b, becauser<5/12<3/4; if the right physical weights satisfyu_i>=h_i, the parent weights do too, sincetau>1 andtheleftweights are exactlyh_i.

## 4. The actual leaf inputs are available

For a single step1<h<=sqrt2, the exact history identity is

    E_(h(h+1))=(h+1)Q_01+Q_10+(2-h²)||g0-g1||²>=0.

HereE_Gamma=2h(f0-f1)-h(h-1)||g0||²-Gamma||g1||². DecreasingGamma preserves nonnegativity. This supplies(1) withGamma=(7/10)(1+h)²-(1+h), becauseh(h+1)-Gamma=(3/10)(1+h)².

The actual inherited leaf has1<h<sqrt2. For its weak dominance totalB_1=1+2kappa h, chooseweightsu0=h,u1=1+(2kappa-1)h. Their residual equalsE_(u1(u1-1)). Since0<2kappa-1<1,

    h(h+1)-u1(u1-1)
       =h[(1-sigma)+(1-sigma²)h]>=0,
    sigma=2kappa-1.

Thus this is also a genuine full-class leaf dominance input. No claim about higher history inputs follows from the leaf.

## 5. Precise unresolved all-depth premise

If, for every sufficiently large enclosingm and EVERY1<=j<m, the actual same-parameterH_j obeyed(1) with

    Gamma_j=(7/10)(1+S_j)²-(1+S_j),

on every full smooth-convex history, then(4)--(11) and the genuine leaf would recursively produce fixed nonnegative parent vectors with total1+(32/17)S_m. This would establish the full original-radius boundW(H_m)<=1/[1+(32/17)S_m], hence the original negative resolution through accepted931b9653ce0f7201.

That left history premise is NOT proved here. The old positive A/D interval cone cannot prove it under its diagonal-slack restrictions, regardless of terminalGamma. The R7 q_j potential was false at its stated stronger coefficients; it does not settle this new all-depth input. A successful next step must supply a genuinely non-diagonal/original-row proof or an actual all-depth obstruction to the displayedGamma_j statement, rather than rename it or tune a scalar without a composition proof.

The advantage established by this note is only the conditional join: it tolerates a three-tenths C² loss in the left terminal-gradient coefficient while retaining the true right dominance residual and a uniform positive quadratic margin. The missing input still has substantial mathematical content. No original-target result, formal acceptance or new resource allowance is claimed.
